# from common/scripted_effects/01_financial_scripted_effects.txt
ai_buy_bond_1 = {
    root = {
        save_scope_as = buyer
    }

    #random_in_global_list = {
    #    variable = central_bank_debt_variable_list_ordered_1
    #    save_scope_as = seller
    #}

	random_country = {
		limit = {
			has_central_bank_SS_BS_GS_GES_NISO = yes #has bc
			has_financial_center = yes #has fc
			remaining_debt > 0
			zz_ef_bl_room > 0
			market_owner_is_root_4 = yes
			central_bank_metal_reserves_state_to_ensure_the_conversion_pourcent < 0.75
			not = {
				has_modifier = country_credit_rating_D
			}
		}
		save_scope_as = seller
	}

    if = {
        limit = {
            scope:buyer.var:ai_selle_bond_maturity_yers_time_1 = 0
            exists = scope:seller
            scope:seller = {
                not = {
                    this = root
                }
            }
            gold_reserves_var > gold_reserves_10 #reserve sup à 10% de la reserve
			#bond_quantity_scripted >  0
        }

        random_list = {
            1 = { #5Years
                scope:seller = {
                    scope:buyer = {
                        # Pour les liste du vendeur
                        add_to_variable_list = {
                            name = ai_seller_country_general_1
                            target = scope:seller #prev
                        }

						#seller
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_seller_country_general_1
                            value = scope:seller
                        }

                       #seller save scope
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_seller_country_general_1
                            value = scope:seller
                        }

                        ######
                        #quantité
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_bond_quantity_1
                            value = bond_quantity_scripted
                        }
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_quantity_purchasing_1
                            value = var:ai_bond_quantity_1
                        }

                        #interret lors de l'achat
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_purchasing_1
                            value = scope:seller.interest_value_5Y
                        }

                        #valeur de la quantité
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_total_bond_value_1
                            value = 1
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_total_bond_value_1
                            multiply = var:ai_bond_quantity_1 #bond quantité
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_total_bond_value_1
                            multiply = scope:seller.bond_price
                        }

                        #interet total
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_1
                            value = 1
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_1
                            multiply = var:ai_bond_quantity_1 #bond quantité
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_1
                            multiply = scope:seller.bond_price
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_1
                            multiply = scope:seller.interest_value_5Y
                        }

                        #interet total à terme
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_at_term_calculated_1
                            value = var:ai_interest_calculated_1
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_at_term_calculated_1
                            multiply = 5
                        }

                        #interet par mois
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_per_month_calculated_1
                            value = var:ai_interest_calculated_1
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_per_month_calculated_1
                            divide = 12
                        }

                        add_treasury = {
                            subtract = var:ai_total_bond_value_1
                        }
                        # the treasury's purchase of a foreign bond -- ours (the ledger registers the part)
                        zz_ef_tr_unmark = { V = var:ai_total_bond_value_1 }

                        interest_per_month_from_foreign_debt_investment = yes

                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_maturity_time_1
                            value = 5
                        }

                        #demare le on_action
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_buy_bond_1_on
                            value = 1
                        }
                    }

                    #################
                    #notification
                    every_country = {
                        limit  = {
                            is_player = yes
                        }
                        save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
                    }
                    if = {
                        limit = {
                            scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
                        }
                        scope:is_player_for_notification = {
                            post_notification = ai_selle_bond_maturity_1_message
                        }
                    }

                    #################
                    #ajout à la liste des acheteur
                    add_to_variable_list = {
                        name = central_bank_debt_buyer_list_1
                        target = scope:buyer
                    }
                }
            }
            1 = { #10Years
                scope:seller = {
                    scope:buyer = {
                        # Pour les liste du vendeur
                        add_to_variable_list = {
                            name = ai_seller_country_general_1
                            target = scope:seller #prev
                        }

                       #seller save scope
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_seller_country_general_1
                            value = scope:seller
                        }

                        ######
                        #quantité
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_bond_quantity_1
                            value = bond_quantity_scripted
                        }
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_quantity_purchasing_1
                            value = var:ai_bond_quantity_1
                        }

                        #interret lors de l'achat
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_purchasing_1
                            value = scope:seller.interest_value_10Y
                        }

                        #valeur de la quantité
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_total_bond_value_1
                            value = 1
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_total_bond_value_1
                            multiply = var:ai_bond_quantity_1 #bond quantité
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_total_bond_value_1
                            multiply = scope:seller.bond_price
                        }

                        #interet total
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_1
                            value = 1
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_1
                            multiply = var:ai_bond_quantity_1 #bond quantité
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_1
                            multiply = scope:seller.bond_price
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_1
                            multiply = scope:seller.interest_value_10Y
                        }

                        #interet total à terme
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_at_term_calculated_1
                            value = var:ai_interest_calculated_1
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_at_term_calculated_1
                            multiply = 10
                        }

                        #interet par mois
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_per_month_calculated_1
                            value = var:ai_interest_calculated_1
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_per_month_calculated_1
                            divide = 12
                        }

                        add_treasury = {
                            subtract = var:ai_total_bond_value_1
                        }
                        # the treasury's purchase of a foreign bond -- ours (the ledger registers the part)
                        zz_ef_tr_unmark = { V = var:ai_total_bond_value_1 }

                        interest_per_month_from_foreign_debt_investment = yes

                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_maturity_time_1
                            value = 10
                        }

                        #demare le on_action
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_buy_bond_1_on
                            value = 1
                        }
                    }

                    #################
                    #notification
                    every_country = {
                        limit  = {
                            is_player = yes
                        }
                        save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
                    }
                    if = {
                        limit = {
                            scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
                        }
                        scope:is_player_for_notification = {
                            post_notification = ai_selle_bond_maturity_1_message
                        }
                    }

                    #################
                    #ajout à la liste des acheteur
                    add_to_variable_list = {
                        name = central_bank_debt_buyer_list_1
                        target = scope:buyer
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

    root = {
        save_scope_as = buyer
    }

    #    variable = central_bank_debt_variable_list_ordered_1
    #    save_scope_as = seller
    #}

	random_country = {
		limit = {
			has_central_bank_SS_BS_GS_GES_NISO = yes #has bc
			has_financial_center = yes #has fc
			remaining_debt > 0
			zz_ef_bl_room > 0
			market_owner_is_root_4 = yes
			central_bank_metal_reserves_state_to_ensure_the_conversion_pourcent < 0.75
			not = {
				has_modifier = country_credit_rating_D
			}
		}
		save_scope_as = seller
	}

    if = {

                not = {

			#bond_quantity_scripted >  0

        random_list = {
            1 = { #5Years
                scope:seller = {
                    scope:buyer = {

                    }

                    #################
                    #ajout à la liste des acheteur
                    add_to_variable_list = {
                        name = central_bank_debt_buyer_list_3
                        target = scope:buyer
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
ai_buy_bond_4 = {
    root = {
        save_scope_as = buyer
    }

    #random_in_global_list = {
    #    variable = central_bank_debt_variable_list_ordered_1
    #    save_scope_as = seller
    #}

	random_country = {
		limit = {
			has_central_bank_SS_BS_GS_GES_NISO = yes #has bc
			has_financial_center = yes #has fc
			remaining_debt > 0
			zz_ef_bl_room > 0
			market_owner_is_root_4 = yes
			central_bank_metal_reserves_state_to_ensure_the_conversion_pourcent < 0.75
			not = {
				has_modifier = country_credit_rating_D
			}
		}
		save_scope_as = seller
	}

    if = {
        limit = {
            scope:buyer.var:ai_selle_bond_maturity_yers_time_4 = 0
            exists = scope:seller
            scope:seller = {
                not = {
                    this = root
                }
            }
            gold_reserves_var > gold_reserves_10 #reserve sup à 10% de la reserve
			#bond_quantity_scripted >  0
        }

        random_list = {
            1 = { #5Years
                scope:seller = {
                    scope:buyer = {
                        # Pour les liste du vendeur
                        add_to_variable_list = {
                            name = ai_seller_country_general_4
                            target = scope:seller #prev
                        }

						#seller
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_seller_country_general_4
                            value = scope:seller
                        }

                       #seller save scope
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_seller_country_general_4
                            value = scope:seller
                        }

                        ######
                        #quantité
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_bond_quantity_4
                            value = bond_quantity_scripted
                        }
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_quantity_purchasing_4
                            value = var:ai_bond_quantity_4
                        }

                        #interret lors de l'achat
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_purchasing_4
                            value = scope:seller.interest_value_5Y
                        }

                        #valeur de la quantité
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_total_bond_value_4
                            value = 1
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_total_bond_value_4
                            multiply = var:ai_bond_quantity_4 #bond quantité
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_total_bond_value_4
                            multiply = scope:seller.bond_price
                        }

                        #interet total
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_4
                            value = 1
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_4
                            multiply = var:ai_bond_quantity_4 #bond quantité
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_4
                            multiply = scope:seller.bond_price
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_4
                            multiply = scope:seller.interest_value_5Y
                        }

                        #interet total à terme
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_at_term_calculated_4
                            value = var:ai_interest_calculated_4
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_at_term_calculated_4
                            multiply = 5
                        }

                        #interet par mois
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_per_month_calculated_4
                            value = var:ai_interest_calculated_4
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_per_month_calculated_4
                            divide = 12
                        }

                        add_treasury = {
                            subtract = var:ai_total_bond_value_4
                        }
                        # the treasury's purchase of a foreign bond -- ours (the ledger registers the part)
                        zz_ef_tr_unmark = { V = var:ai_total_bond_value_4 }

                        interest_per_month_from_foreign_debt_investment = yes

                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_maturity_time_4
                            value = 5
                        }

                        #demare le on_action
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_buy_bond_4_on
                            value = 1
                        }
                    }

                    #################
                    #notification
                    every_country = {
                        limit  = {
                            is_player = yes
                        }
                        save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
                    }
                    if = {
                        limit = {
                            scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
                        }
                        scope:is_player_for_notification = {
                            post_notification = ai_selle_bond_maturity_4_message
                        }
                    }

                    #################
                    #ajout à la liste des acheteur
                    add_to_variable_list = {
                        name = central_bank_debt_buyer_list_4
                        target = scope:buyer
                    }
                }
            }
            1 = { #10Years
                scope:seller = {
                    scope:buyer = {
                        # Pour les liste du vendeur
                        add_to_variable_list = {
                            name = ai_seller_country_general_4
                            target = scope:seller #prev
                        }

                       #seller save scope
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_seller_country_general_4
                            value = scope:seller
                        }

                        ######
                        #quantité
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_bond_quantity_4
                            value = bond_quantity_scripted
                        }
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_quantity_purchasing_4
                            value = var:ai_bond_quantity_4
                        }

                        #interret lors de l'achat
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_purchasing_4
                            value = scope:seller.interest_value_10Y
                        }

                        #valeur de la quantité
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_total_bond_value_4
                            value = 1
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_total_bond_value_4
                            multiply = var:ai_bond_quantity_4 #bond quantité
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_total_bond_value_4
                            multiply = scope:seller.bond_price
                        }

                        #interet total
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_4
                            value = 1
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_4
                            multiply = var:ai_bond_quantity_4 #bond quantité
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_4
                            multiply = scope:seller.bond_price
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_4
                            multiply = scope:seller.interest_value_10Y
                        }

                        #interet total à terme
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_at_term_calculated_4
                            value = var:ai_interest_calculated_4
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_at_term_calculated_4
                            multiply = 10
                        }

                        #interet par mois
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_per_month_calculated_4
                            value = var:ai_interest_calculated_4
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_per_month_calculated_4
                            divide = 12
                        }

                        add_treasury = {
                            subtract = var:ai_total_bond_value_4
                        }
                        # the treasury's purchase of a foreign bond -- ours (the ledger registers the part)
                        zz_ef_tr_unmark = { V = var:ai_total_bond_value_4 }

                        interest_per_month_from_foreign_debt_investment = yes

                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_maturity_time_4
                            value = 10
                        }

                        #demare le on_action
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_buy_bond_4_on
                            value = 1
                        }
                    }

                    #################
                    #notification
                    every_country = {
                        limit  = {
                            is_player = yes
                        }
                        save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
                    }
                    if = {
                        limit = {
                            scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
                        }
                        scope:is_player_for_notification = {
                            post_notification = ai_selle_bond_maturity_4_message
                        }
                    }

                    #################
                    #ajout à la liste des acheteur
                    add_to_variable_list = {
                        name = central_bank_debt_buyer_list_4
                        target = scope:buyer
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

                            scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
                        }
                        scope:is_player_for_notification = {
                            post_notification = ai_selle_bond_maturity_6_message
                        }
                    }

                    #################
                    #ajout à la liste des acheteur
                    add_to_variable_list = {
                        name = central_bank_debt_buyer_list_6
                        target = scope:buyer
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
ai_buy_bond_7 = {
    root = {
        save_scope_as = buyer
    }

    #random_in_global_list = {
    #    variable = central_bank_debt_variable_list_ordered_1
    #    save_scope_as = seller
    #}

	random_country = {
		limit = {
			has_central_bank_SS_BS_GS_GES_NISO = yes #has bc
			has_financial_center = yes #has fc
			remaining_debt > 0
			zz_ef_bl_room > 0
			market_owner_is_root_4 = yes
			central_bank_metal_reserves_state_to_ensure_the_conversion_pourcent < 0.75
			not = {
				has_modifier = country_credit_rating_D
			}
		}
		save_scope_as = seller
	}

    if = {
        limit = {
            scope:buyer.var:ai_selle_bond_maturity_yers_time_7 = 0
            exists = scope:seller
            scope:seller = {
                not = {
                    this = root
                }
            }
            gold_reserves_var > gold_reserves_10 #reserve sup à 10% de la reserve
			#bond_quantity_scripted >  0
        }

        random_list = {
            1 = { #5Years
                scope:seller = {
                    scope:buyer = {
                        # Pour les liste du vendeur
                        add_to_variable_list = {
                            name = ai_seller_country_general_7
                            target = scope:seller #prev
                        }

						#seller
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_seller_country_general_7
                            value = scope:seller
                        }

                       #seller save scope
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_seller_country_general_7
                            value = scope:seller
                        }

                        ######
                        #quantité
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_bond_quantity_7
                            value = bond_quantity_scripted
                        }
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_quantity_purchasing_7
                            value = var:ai_bond_quantity_7
                        }

                        #interret lors de l'achat
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_purchasing_7
                            value = scope:seller.interest_value_5Y
                        }

                        #valeur de la quantité
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_total_bond_value_7
                            value = 1
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_total_bond_value_7
                            multiply = var:ai_bond_quantity_7 #bond quantité
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_total_bond_value_7
                            multiply = scope:seller.bond_price
                        }

                        #interet total
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_7
                            value = 1
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_7
                            multiply = var:ai_bond_quantity_7 #bond quantité
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_7
                            multiply = scope:seller.bond_price
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_7
                            multiply = scope:seller.interest_value_5Y
                        }

                        #interet total à terme
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_at_term_calculated_7
                            value = var:ai_interest_calculated_7
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_at_term_calculated_7
                            multiply = 5
                        }

                        #interet par mois
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_per_month_calculated_7
                            value = var:ai_interest_calculated_7
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_per_month_calculated_7
                            divide = 12
                        }

                        add_treasury = {
                            subtract = var:ai_total_bond_value_7
                        }
                        # the treasury's purchase of a foreign bond -- ours (the ledger registers the part)
                        zz_ef_tr_unmark = { V = var:ai_total_bond_value_7 }

                        interest_per_month_from_foreign_debt_investment = yes

                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_maturity_time_7
                            value = 5
                        }

                        #demare le on_action
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_buy_bond_7_on
                            value = 1
                        }
                    }

                    #################
                    #notification
                    every_country = {
                        limit  = {
                            is_player = yes
                        }
                        save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
                    }
                    if = {
                        limit = {
                            scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
                        }
                        scope:is_player_for_notification = {
                            post_notification = ai_selle_bond_maturity_7_message
                        }
                    }

                    #################
                    #ajout à la liste des acheteur
                    add_to_variable_list = {
                        name = central_bank_debt_buyer_list_7
                        target = scope:buyer
                    }
                }
            }
            1 = { #10Years
                scope:seller = {
                    scope:buyer = {
                        # Pour les liste du vendeur
                        add_to_variable_list = {
                            name = ai_seller_country_general_7
                            target = scope:seller #prev
                        }

                       #seller save scope
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_seller_country_general_7
                            value = scope:seller
                        }

                        ######
                        #quantité
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_bond_quantity_7
                            value = bond_quantity_scripted
                        }
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_quantity_purchasing_7
                            value = var:ai_bond_quantity_7
                        }

                        #interret lors de l'achat
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_purchasing_7
                            value = scope:seller.interest_value_10Y
                        }

                        #valeur de la quantité
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_total_bond_value_7
                            value = 1
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_total_bond_value_7
                            multiply = var:ai_bond_quantity_7 #bond quantité
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_total_bond_value_7
                            multiply = scope:seller.bond_price
                        }

                        #interet total
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_7
                            value = 1
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_7
                            multiply = var:ai_bond_quantity_7 #bond quantité
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_7
                            multiply = scope:seller.bond_price
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_7
                            multiply = scope:seller.interest_value_10Y
                        }

                        #interet total à terme
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_at_term_calculated_7
                            value = var:ai_interest_calculated_7
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_at_term_calculated_7
                            multiply = 10
                        }

                        #interet par mois
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_per_month_calculated_7
                            value = var:ai_interest_calculated_7
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_per_month_calculated_7
                            divide = 12
                        }

                        add_treasury = {
                            subtract = var:ai_total_bond_value_7
                        }
                        # the treasury's purchase of a foreign bond -- ours (the ledger registers the part)
                        zz_ef_tr_unmark = { V = var:ai_total_bond_value_7 }

                        interest_per_month_from_foreign_debt_investment = yes

                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_maturity_time_7
                            value = 10
                        }

                        #demare le on_action
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_buy_bond_7_on
                            value = 1
                        }
                    }

                    #################
                    #notification
                    every_country = {
                        limit  = {
                            is_player = yes
                        }
                        save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
                    }
                    if = {
                        limit = {
                            scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
                        }
                        scope:is_player_for_notification = {
                            post_notification = ai_selle_bond_maturity_7_message
                        }
                    }

                    #################
                    #ajout à la liste des acheteur
                    add_to_variable_list = {
                        name = central_bank_debt_buyer_list_7
                        target = scope:buyer
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

                    every_country = {
                        limit  = {
                            is_player = yes
                        }
                        save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
                    }
                    if = {
                        limit = {
                            scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
                        }
                        scope:is_player_for_notification = {
                            post_notification = ai_selle_bond_maturity_9_message
                        }
                    }

                    #################
                    #ajout à la liste des acheteur
                    add_to_variable_list = {
                        name = central_bank_debt_buyer_list_9
                        target = scope:buyer
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
ai_buy_bond_10 = {
    root = {
        save_scope_as = buyer
    }

    #random_in_global_list = {
    #    variable = central_bank_debt_variable_list_ordered_1
    #    save_scope_as = seller
    #}

	random_country = {
		limit = {
			has_central_bank_SS_BS_GS_GES_NISO = yes #has bc
			has_financial_center = yes #has fc
			remaining_debt > 0
			zz_ef_bl_room > 0
			market_owner_is_root_4 = yes
			central_bank_metal_reserves_state_to_ensure_the_conversion_pourcent < 0.75
			not = {
				has_modifier = country_credit_rating_D
			}
		}
		save_scope_as = seller
	}

    if = {
        limit = {
            scope:buyer.var:ai_selle_bond_maturity_yers_time_10 = 0
            exists = scope:seller
            scope:seller = {
                not = {
                    this = root
                }
            }
            gold_reserves_var > gold_reserves_10 #reserve sup à 10% de la reserve
			#bond_quantity_scripted >  0
        }

        random_list = {
            1 = { #5Years
                scope:seller = {
                    scope:buyer = {
                        # Pour les liste du vendeur
                        add_to_variable_list = {
                            name = ai_seller_country_general_10
                            target = scope:seller #prev
                        }

						#seller
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_seller_country_general_10
                            value = scope:seller
                        }

                       #seller save scope
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_seller_country_general_10
                            value = scope:seller
                        }

                        ######
                        #quantité
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_bond_quantity_10
                            value = bond_quantity_scripted
                        }
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_quantity_purchasing_10
                            value = var:ai_bond_quantity_10
                        }

                        #interret lors de l'achat
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_purchasing_10
                            value = scope:seller.interest_value_5Y
                        }

                        #valeur de la quantité
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_total_bond_value_10
                            value = 1
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_total_bond_value_10
                            multiply = var:ai_bond_quantity_10 #bond quantité
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_total_bond_value_10
                            multiply = scope:seller.bond_price
                        }

                        #interet total
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_10
                            value = 1
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_10
                            multiply = var:ai_bond_quantity_10 #bond quantité
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_10
                            multiply = scope:seller.bond_price
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_10
                            multiply = scope:seller.interest_value_5Y
                        }

                        #interet total à terme
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_at_term_calculated_10
                            value = var:ai_interest_calculated_10
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_at_term_calculated_10
                            multiply = 5
                        }

                        #interet par mois
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_per_month_calculated_10
                            value = var:ai_interest_calculated_10
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_per_month_calculated_10
                            divide = 12
                        }

                        add_treasury = {
                            subtract = var:ai_total_bond_value_10
                        }
                        # the treasury's purchase of a foreign bond -- ours (the ledger registers the part)
                        zz_ef_tr_unmark = { V = var:ai_total_bond_value_10 }

                        interest_per_month_from_foreign_debt_investment = yes

                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_maturity_time_10
                            value = 5
                        }

                        #demare le on_action
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_buy_bond_10_on
                            value = 1
                        }
                    }

                    #################
                    #notification
                    every_country = {
                        limit  = {
                            is_player = yes
                        }
                        save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
                    }
                    if = {
                        limit = {
                            scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
                        }
                        scope:is_player_for_notification = {
                            post_notification = ai_selle_bond_maturity_10_message
                        }
                    }

                    #################
                    #ajout à la liste des acheteur
                    add_to_variable_list = {
                        name = central_bank_debt_buyer_list_10
                        target = scope:buyer
                    }
                }
            }
            1 = { #10Years
                scope:seller = {
                    scope:buyer = {
                        # Pour les liste du vendeur
                        add_to_variable_list = {
                            name = ai_seller_country_general_10
                            target = scope:seller #prev
                        }

                       #seller save scope
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_seller_country_general_10
                            value = scope:seller
                        }

                        ######
                        #quantité
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_bond_quantity_10
                            value = bond_quantity_scripted
                        }
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_quantity_purchasing_10
                            value = var:ai_bond_quantity_10
                        }

                        #interret lors de l'achat
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_purchasing_10
                            value = scope:seller.interest_value_10Y
                        }

                        #valeur de la quantité
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_total_bond_value_10
                            value = 1
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_total_bond_value_10
                            multiply = var:ai_bond_quantity_10 #bond quantité
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_total_bond_value_10
                            multiply = scope:seller.bond_price
                        }

                        #interet total
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_10
                            value = 1
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_10
                            multiply = var:ai_bond_quantity_10 #bond quantité
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_10
                            multiply = scope:seller.bond_price
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_10
                            multiply = scope:seller.interest_value_10Y
                        }

                        #interet total à terme
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_at_term_calculated_10
                            value = var:ai_interest_calculated_10
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_at_term_calculated_10
                            multiply = 10
                        }

                        #interet par mois
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_per_month_calculated_10
                            value = var:ai_interest_calculated_10
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_per_month_calculated_10
                            divide = 12
                        }

                        add_treasury = {
                            subtract = var:ai_total_bond_value_10
                        }
                        # the treasury's purchase of a foreign bond -- ours (the ledger registers the part)
                        zz_ef_tr_unmark = { V = var:ai_total_bond_value_10 }

                        interest_per_month_from_foreign_debt_investment = yes

                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_maturity_time_10
                            value = 10
                        }

                        #demare le on_action
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_buy_bond_10_on
                            value = 1
                        }
                    }

                    #################
                    #notification
                    every_country = {
                        limit  = {
                            is_player = yes
                        }
                        save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
                    }
                    if = {
                        limit = {
                            scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
                        }
                        scope:is_player_for_notification = {
                            post_notification = ai_selle_bond_maturity_10_message
                        }
                    }

                    #################
                    #ajout à la liste des acheteur
                    add_to_variable_list = {
                        name = central_bank_debt_buyer_list_10
                        target = scope:buyer
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
ai_bond_maturity_1 = {
	#gui timer
	change_variable = {
		name = ai_selle_bond_maturity_yers_time_1
		add = 1
	}
	#maturity
	change_variable = {
		name = ai_selle_bond_maturity_yers_1
		add = 1
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			or = {
				var:ai_selle_bond_maturity_yers_1 = var:ai_maturity_time_1
				ai_no_longer_exists_1 = 0 #no_longer_exists
			}
		}

		# the payout from nobody removed -- the ledger buys the part back

		#Reset variable
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_buy_bond_1_on
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_interest_purchasing_1
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_bond_quantity_1
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_quantity_purchasing_1
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_selle_bond_maturity_yers_time_1
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_interest_calculated_1
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_total_bond_value_1
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_interest_at_term_calculated_1
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_interest_per_month_calculated_1
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_selle_bond_maturity_yers_1
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_seller_country_general_1
			value = 0
		}

		#seller
		every_in_list = {
			variable = ai_seller_country_general_1
			central_bank_debt_buyer_list_1_clear = yes
			#save_scope_as = seller
		}
		# scope:seller = {
		# 	central_bank_debt_buyer_list_1_clear = yes
		# }

		#Reset list

		clear_variable_list = ai_seller_country_general_1
		interest_per_month_from_foreign_debt_investment = yes
	}
}

			name = ai_interest_per_month_calculated_7
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_selle_bond_maturity_yers_7
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_seller_country_general_7
			value = 0
		}

		#seller
		every_in_list = {
			variable = ai_seller_country_general_7
			central_bank_debt_buyer_list_7_clear = yes
			#save_scope_as = seller
		}
		# scope:seller = {
		# 	central_bank_debt_buyer_list_7_clear = yes
		# }

		#Reset list

		clear_variable_list = ai_seller_country_general_7
		interest_per_month_from_foreign_debt_investment = yes
	}
}
ai_bond_maturity_8 = {
	#gui timer
	change_variable = {
		name = ai_selle_bond_maturity_yers_time_8
		add = 1
	}
	#maturity
	change_variable = {
		name = ai_selle_bond_maturity_yers_8
		add = 1
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			or = {
				var:ai_selle_bond_maturity_yers_8 = var:ai_maturity_time_8
				ai_no_longer_exists_8 = 0 #no_longer_exists
			}
		}

		# the payout from nobody removed -- the ledger buys the part back

		#Reset variable
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_buy_bond_8_on
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_interest_purchasing_8
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_bond_quantity_8
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_quantity_purchasing_8
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_selle_bond_maturity_yers_time_8
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_interest_calculated_8
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_total_bond_value_8

		set_variable = {

		set_variable = {

ai_privat_bank_bond_1 = {
    root = {
        save_scope_as = buyer
    }

	random_in_global_list = {
		variable = central_bank_debt_variable_list_ordered_1
		save_scope_as = seller
	}


	random_in_list = {
		variable = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_1
		save_scope_as = scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_1
	}
	random_in_list = {
		variable = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_2
		save_scope_as = scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_2
	}
	random_in_list = {
		variable = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_3
		save_scope_as = scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_3
	}
	random_in_list = {
		variable = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_4
		save_scope_as = scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_4
	}
	random_in_list = {
		variable = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_5
		save_scope_as = scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_5
	}
	random_in_list = {
		variable = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_6
		save_scope_as = scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_6
	}
	random_in_list = {
		variable = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_7
		save_scope_as = scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_7
	}
	random_in_list = {
		variable = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_8
		save_scope_as = scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_8
	}
	random_in_list = {
		variable = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_9
		save_scope_as = scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_9
	}
	random_in_list = {
		variable = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_10
		save_scope_as = scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_10
	}
	random_in_list = {
		variable = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_11
		save_scope_as = scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_11
	}
	random_in_list = {
		variable = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_12
		save_scope_as = scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_12
	}
	random_in_list = {
		variable = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_13
		save_scope_as = scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_13
	}
	random_in_list = {
		variable = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_14
		save_scope_as = scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_14
	}
	random_in_list = {
		variable = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_15
		save_scope_as = scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_15
	}
	random_in_list = {
		variable = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_16
		save_scope_as = scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_16
	}
	random_in_list = {
		variable = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_17
		save_scope_as = scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_17
	}
	random_in_list = {
		variable = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_18
		save_scope_as = scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_18
	}
	random_in_list = {
		variable = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_19
		save_scope_as = scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_19
	}
	random_in_list = {
		variable = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_20
		save_scope_as = scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_20
	}
	random_in_list = {
		variable = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_21
		save_scope_as = scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_21
	}
	random_in_list = {
		variable = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_22
		save_scope_as = scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_22
	}
	random_in_list = {
		variable = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_23
		save_scope_as = scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_23
	}
	random_in_list = {
		variable = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_24
		save_scope_as = scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_24
	}
	random_in_list = {
		variable = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_25
		save_scope_as = scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_25
	}

    if = {
        limit = {
            exists = scope:seller
			not = {
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_1 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_1 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_2 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_2 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_3 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_3 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_4 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_4 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_5 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_5 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_6 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_6 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_7 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_7 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_8 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_8 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_9 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_9 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_10 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_10 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_11 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_11 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_12 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_12 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_13 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_13 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_14 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_14 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_15 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_15 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_16 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_16 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_17 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_17 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_18 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_18 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_19 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_19 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_20 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_20 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_21 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_21 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_22 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_22 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_23 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_23 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_24 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_24 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_25 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_25 }
			}
        }
		scope:seller = {
			scope:buyer = {
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_slot_1
					value = 1 #slot pris
				}

				#selection de la bank
				random_in_list = {
					variable = privat_bank_variable_list_ordered
     			   	save_scope_as = buyer_privat_bank
				}

				# Pour les liste du vendeur
				add_to_variable_list = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_country_1
					target = scope:buyer_privat_bank #prev
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_1
					target = scope:seller #prev
				}

				######
				#quantité
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_1
					value = bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank
				}

				#valeur de la quantité
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_1
					value = 1
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_1
					multiply = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_1 #bond quantité
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_1
					multiply = scope:seller.bond_price_in_gold
				}

				#interet total
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_1
					value = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_1
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_1
					multiply = scope:seller.interest_value_10Y
				}
				add_investment_pool = {
					subtract = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_1
				}
			}

			#################
			#notification
			every_country = {
				limit  = {
					is_player = yes
				}
				save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_selle_bond_maturity_1_message
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:buyer = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_buy_bond_maturity_1_message
				}
			}

			#central_bank_interest_refund_per_year_in_gold = yes

			#################
			#ajout à la liste des acheteur
			add_to_variable_list = {
				name =  central_bank_debt_privat_bank_buyer_list_1 #plusieur bank peuvent etre stack dans cette liste
				target = scope:buyer_privat_bank
			}
		}
    }
}

		save_scope_as = scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_24
	}
	random_in_list = {
		variable = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_25
		save_scope_as = scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_25
	}

    if = {
        limit = {
            exists = scope:seller
			not = {
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_1 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_1 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_2 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_2 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_3 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_3 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_4 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_4 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_5 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_5 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_6 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_6 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_7 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_7 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_8 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_8 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_9 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_9 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_10 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_10 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_11 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_11 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_12 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_12 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_13 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_13 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_14 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_14 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_15 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_15 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_16 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_16 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_17 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_17 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_18 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_18 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_19 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_19 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_20 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_20 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_21 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_21 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_22 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_22 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_23 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_23 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_24 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_24 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_25 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_25 }
			}
        }
		scope:seller = {
			scope:buyer = {
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_slot_2
					value = 1 #slot pris
				}

				#selection de la bank
				random_in_list = {
					variable = privat_bank_variable_list_ordered
     			   	save_scope_as = buyer_privat_bank
				}

				# Pour les liste du vendeur
				add_to_variable_list = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_country_2
					target = scope:buyer_privat_bank #prev
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_2
					target = scope:seller #prev
				}

				######
				#quantité
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_2
					value = bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank
				}

				#valeur de la quantité
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_2
					value = 1
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_2
					multiply = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_2 #bond quantité
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_2
					multiply = scope:seller.bond_price_in_gold
				}

				#interet total
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_2
					value = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_2
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_2
					multiply = scope:seller.interest_value_10Y
				}
				add_investment_pool = {
					subtract = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_2
				}
			}

			#################
			#notification
			every_country = {
				limit  = {
					is_player = yes
				}
				save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_selle_bond_maturity_2_message
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:buyer = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_buy_bond_maturity_2_message
				}
			}

			#central_bank_interest_refund_per_year_in_gold = yes

			#################
			#ajout à la liste des acheteur
			add_to_variable_list = {
				name =  central_bank_debt_privat_bank_buyer_list_2 #plusieur bank peuvent etre stack dans cette liste
				target = scope:buyer_privat_bank
			}
		}
    }
}

			not = {
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_1 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_1 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_2 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_2 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_3 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_3 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_4 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_4 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_5 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_5 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_6 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_6 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_7 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_7 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_8 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_8 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_9 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_9 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_10 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_10 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_11 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_11 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_12 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_12 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_13 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_13 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_14 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_14 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_15 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_15 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_16 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_16 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_17 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_17 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_18 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_18 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_19 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_19 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_20 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_20 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_21 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_21 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_22 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_22 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_23 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_23 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_24 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_24 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_25 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_25 }
			}
        }
		scope:seller = {
			scope:buyer = {
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_slot_4
					value = 1 #slot pris
				}

				#selection de la bank
				random_in_list = {
					variable = privat_bank_variable_list_ordered
     			   	save_scope_as = buyer_privat_bank
				}

				# Pour les liste du vendeur
				add_to_variable_list = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_country_4
					target = scope:buyer_privat_bank #prev
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_4
					target = scope:seller #prev
				}

				######
				#quantité
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_4
					value = bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank
				}

				#valeur de la quantité
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_4
					value = 1
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_4
					multiply = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_4 #bond quantité
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_4
					multiply = scope:seller.bond_price_in_gold
				}

				#interet total
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_4
					value = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_4
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_4
					multiply = scope:seller.interest_value_10Y
				}
				add_investment_pool = {
					subtract = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_4
				}
			}

			#################
			#notification
			every_country = {
				limit  = {
					is_player = yes
				}
				save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_selle_bond_maturity_4_message
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:buyer = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_buy_bond_maturity_4_message
				}
			}

			#central_bank_interest_refund_per_year_in_gold = yes

			#################
			#ajout à la liste des acheteur
			add_to_variable_list = {
				name =  central_bank_debt_privat_bank_buyer_list_4 #plusieur bank peuvent etre stack dans cette liste
				target = scope:buyer_privat_bank
			}
		}
    }
}

			not = {

				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_3 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_3 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_4 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_4 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_5 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_5 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_6 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_6 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_7 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_7 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_8 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_8 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_9 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_9 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_10 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_10 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_11 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_11 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_12 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_12 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_13 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_13 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_14 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_14 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_15 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_15 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_16 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_16 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_17 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_17 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_18 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_18 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_19 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_19 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_20 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_20 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_21 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_21 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_22 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_22 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_23 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_23 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_24 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_24 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_25 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_25 }
			}
        }
		scope:seller = {
			scope:buyer = {
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_slot_6
					value = 1 #slot pris
				}

				#selection de la bank
				random_in_list = {
					variable = privat_bank_variable_list_ordered
     			   	save_scope_as = buyer_privat_bank
				}

				# Pour les liste du vendeur
				add_to_variable_list = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_country_6
					target = scope:buyer_privat_bank #prev
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_6
					target = scope:seller #prev
				}

				######
				#quantité
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_6
					value = bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank
				}

				#valeur de la quantité
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_6
					value = 1
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_6
					multiply = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_6 #bond quantité
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_6
					multiply = scope:seller.bond_price_in_gold
				}

				#interet total
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_6
					value = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_6
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_6
					multiply = scope:seller.interest_value_10Y
				}
				add_investment_pool = {
					subtract = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_6
				}
			}

			#################
			#notification
			every_country = {
				limit  = {
					is_player = yes
				}
				save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_selle_bond_maturity_6_message
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:buyer = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_buy_bond_maturity_6_message
				}
			}

			#central_bank_interest_refund_per_year_in_gold = yes

			#################
			#ajout à la liste des acheteur
			add_to_variable_list = {
				name =  central_bank_debt_privat_bank_buyer_list_6 #plusieur bank peuvent etre stack dans cette liste
				target = scope:buyer_privat_bank
			}
		}
    }
}
