				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_2 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_2 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_3 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_3 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_4 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_4 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_5 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_5 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_6 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_6 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_7 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_7 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_8 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_8 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_9 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_9 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_10 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_10 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_11 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_11 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_12 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_12 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_13 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_13 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_14 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_14 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_15 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_15 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_16 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_16 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_17 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_17 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_18 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_18 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_19 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_19 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_20 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_20 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_21 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_21 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_22 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_22 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_23 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_23 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_24 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_24 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_25 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_25 }
			}
        }
		scope:seller = {
			scope:buyer = {
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_slot_8
					value = 1 #slot pris
				}

				#selection de la bank
				random_in_list = {
					variable = privat_bank_variable_list_ordered
     			   	save_scope_as = buyer_privat_bank
				}

				# Pour les liste du vendeur
				add_to_variable_list = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_country_8
					target = scope:buyer_privat_bank #prev
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_8
					target = scope:seller #prev
				}

				######
				#quantité
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_8
					value = bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank
				}

				#valeur de la quantité
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_8
					value = 1
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_8
					multiply = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_8 #bond quantité
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_8
					multiply = scope:seller.bond_price_in_gold
				}

				#interet total
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_8
					value = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_8
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_8
					multiply = scope:seller.interest_value_10Y
				}
				add_investment_pool = {
					subtract = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_8
				}
			}

			#################
			#notification
			every_country = {
				limit  = {
					is_player = yes
				}
				save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_selle_bond_maturity_8_message
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:buyer = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_buy_bond_maturity_8_message
				}
			}

			#central_bank_interest_refund_per_year_in_gold = yes

			#################
			#ajout à la liste des acheteur
			add_to_variable_list = {
				name =  central_bank_debt_privat_bank_buyer_list_8 #plusieur bank peuvent etre stack dans cette liste
				target = scope:buyer_privat_bank
			}
		}
    }
}

				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_4 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_4 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_5 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_5 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_6 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_6 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_7 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_7 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_8 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_8 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_9 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_9 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_10 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_10 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_11 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_11 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_12 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_12 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_13 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_13 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_14 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_14 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_15 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_15 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_16 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_16 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_17 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_17 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_18 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_18 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_19 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_19 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_20 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_20 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_21 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_21 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_22 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_22 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_23 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_23 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_24 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_24 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_25 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_25 }
			}
        }
		scope:seller = {
			scope:buyer = {
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_slot_10
					value = 1 #slot pris
				}

				#selection de la bank
				random_in_list = {
					variable = privat_bank_variable_list_ordered
     			   	save_scope_as = buyer_privat_bank
				}

				# Pour les liste du vendeur
				add_to_variable_list = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_country_10
					target = scope:buyer_privat_bank #prev
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_10
					target = scope:seller #prev
				}

				######
				#quantité
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_10
					value = bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank
				}

				#valeur de la quantité
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_10
					value = 1
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_10
					multiply = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_10 #bond quantité
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_10
					multiply = scope:seller.bond_price_in_gold
				}

				#interet total
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_10
					value = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_10
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_10
					multiply = scope:seller.interest_value_10Y
				}
				add_investment_pool = {
					subtract = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_10
				}
			}

			#################
			#notification
			every_country = {
				limit  = {
					is_player = yes
				}
				save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_selle_bond_maturity_10_message
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:buyer = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_buy_bond_maturity_10_message
				}
			}

			#central_bank_interest_refund_per_year_in_gold = yes

			#################
			#ajout à la liste des acheteur
			add_to_variable_list = {
				name =  central_bank_debt_privat_bank_buyer_list_10 #plusieur bank peuvent etre stack dans cette liste
				target = scope:buyer_privat_bank
			}
		}
    }
}

				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_4 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_4 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_5 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_5 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_6 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_6 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_7 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_7 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_8 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_8 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_9 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_9 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_10 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_10 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_11 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_11 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_12 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_12 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_13 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_13 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_14 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_14 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_15 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_15 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_16 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_16 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_17 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_17 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_18 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_18 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_19 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_19 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_20 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_20 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_21 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_21 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_22 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_22 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_23 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_23 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_24 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_24 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_25 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_25 }
			}
        }
		scope:seller = {
			scope:buyer = {
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_slot_12
					value = 1 #slot pris
				}

				#selection de la bank
				random_in_list = {
					variable = privat_bank_variable_list_ordered
     			   	save_scope_as = buyer_privat_bank
				}

				# Pour les liste du vendeur
				add_to_variable_list = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_country_12
					target = scope:buyer_privat_bank #prev
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_12
					target = scope:seller #prev
				}

				######
				#quantité
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_12
					value = bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank
				}

				#valeur de la quantité
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_12
					value = 1
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_12
					multiply = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_12 #bond quantité
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_12
					multiply = scope:seller.bond_price_in_gold
				}

				#interet total
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_12
					value = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_12
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_12
					multiply = scope:seller.interest_value_10Y
				}
				add_investment_pool = {
					subtract = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_12
				}
			}

			#################
			#notification
			every_country = {
				limit  = {
					is_player = yes
				}
				save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_selle_bond_maturity_12_message
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:buyer = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_buy_bond_maturity_12_message
				}
			}

			#central_bank_interest_refund_per_year_in_gold = yes

			#################
			#ajout à la liste des acheteur
			add_to_variable_list = {
				name =  central_bank_debt_privat_bank_buyer_list_12 #plusieur bank peuvent etre stack dans cette liste
				target = scope:buyer_privat_bank
			}
		}
    }
}

				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_9 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_9 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_10 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_10 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_11 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_11 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_12 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_12 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_13 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_13 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_14 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_14 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_15 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_15 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_16 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_16 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_17 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_17 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_18 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_18 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_19 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_19 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_20 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_20 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_21 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_21 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_22 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_22 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_23 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_23 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_24 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_24 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_25 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_25 }
			}
        }
		scope:seller = {
			scope:buyer = {
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_slot_14
					value = 1 #slot pris
				}

				#selection de la bank
				random_in_list = {
					variable = privat_bank_variable_list_ordered
     			   	save_scope_as = buyer_privat_bank
				}

				# Pour les liste du vendeur
				add_to_variable_list = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_country_14
					target = scope:buyer_privat_bank #prev
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_14
					target = scope:seller #prev
				}

				######
				#quantité
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_14
					value = bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank
				}

				#valeur de la quantité
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_14
					value = 1
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_14
					multiply = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_14 #bond quantité
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_14
					multiply = scope:seller.bond_price_in_gold
				}

				#interet total
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_14
					value = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_14
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_14
					multiply = scope:seller.interest_value_10Y
				}
				add_investment_pool = {
					subtract = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_14
				}
			}

			#################
			#notification
			every_country = {
				limit  = {
					is_player = yes
				}
				save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_selle_bond_maturity_14_message
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:buyer = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_buy_bond_maturity_14_message
				}
			}

			#central_bank_interest_refund_per_year_in_gold = yes

			#################
			#ajout à la liste des acheteur
			add_to_variable_list = {
				name =  central_bank_debt_privat_bank_buyer_list_14 #plusieur bank peuvent etre stack dans cette liste
				target = scope:buyer_privat_bank
			}
		}
    }
}

				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_13 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_13 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_14 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_14 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_15 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_15 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_16 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_16 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_17 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_17 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_18 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_18 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_19 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_19 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_20 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_20 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_21 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_21 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_22 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_22 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_23 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_23 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_24 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_24 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_25 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_25 }
			}
        }
		scope:seller = {
			scope:buyer = {
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_slot_16
					value = 1 #slot pris
				}

				#selection de la bank
				random_in_list = {
					variable = privat_bank_variable_list_ordered
     			   	save_scope_as = buyer_privat_bank
				}

				# Pour les liste du vendeur
				add_to_variable_list = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_country_16
					target = scope:buyer_privat_bank #prev
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_16
					target = scope:seller #prev
				}

				######
				#quantité
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_16
					value = bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank
				}

				#valeur de la quantité
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_16
					value = 1
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_16
					multiply = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_16 #bond quantité
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_16
					multiply = scope:seller.bond_price_in_gold
				}

				#interet total
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_16
					value = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_16
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_16
					multiply = scope:seller.interest_value_10Y
				}
				add_investment_pool = {
					subtract = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_16
				}
			}

			#################
			#notification
			every_country = {
				limit  = {
					is_player = yes
				}
				save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_selle_bond_maturity_16_message
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:buyer = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_buy_bond_maturity_16_message
				}
			}

			#central_bank_interest_refund_per_year_in_gold = yes

			#################
			#ajout à la liste des acheteur
			add_to_variable_list = {
				name =  central_bank_debt_privat_bank_buyer_list_16 #plusieur bank peuvent etre stack dans cette liste
				target = scope:buyer_privat_bank
			}
		}
    }
}

				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_15 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_15 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_16 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_16 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_17 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_17 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_18 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_18 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_19 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_19 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_20 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_20 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_21 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_21 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_22 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_22 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_23 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_23 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_24 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_24 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_25 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_25 }
			}
        }
		scope:seller = {
			scope:buyer = {
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_slot_18
					value = 1 #slot pris
				}

				#selection de la bank
				random_in_list = {
					variable = privat_bank_variable_list_ordered
     			   	save_scope_as = buyer_privat_bank
				}

				# Pour les liste du vendeur
				add_to_variable_list = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_country_18
					target = scope:buyer_privat_bank #prev
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_18
					target = scope:seller #prev
				}

				######
				#quantité
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_18
					value = bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank
				}

				#valeur de la quantité
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_18
					value = 1
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_18
					multiply = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_18 #bond quantité
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_18
					multiply = scope:seller.bond_price_in_gold
				}

				#interet total
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_18
					value = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_18
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_18
					multiply = scope:seller.interest_value_10Y
				}
				add_investment_pool = {
					subtract = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_18
				}
			}

			#################
			#notification
			every_country = {
				limit  = {
					is_player = yes
				}
				save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_selle_bond_maturity_18_message
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:buyer = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_buy_bond_maturity_18_message
				}
			}

			#central_bank_interest_refund_per_year_in_gold = yes

			#################
			#ajout à la liste des acheteur
			add_to_variable_list = {
				name =  central_bank_debt_privat_bank_buyer_list_18 #plusieur bank peuvent etre stack dans cette liste
				target = scope:buyer_privat_bank
			}
		}
    }
}

					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_buy_bond_maturity_19_message

				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_17 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_17 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_18 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_18 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_19 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_19 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_20 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_20 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_21 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_21 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_22 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_22 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_23 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_23 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_24 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_24 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_25 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_25 }
			}
        }
		scope:seller = {
			scope:buyer = {
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_slot_20
					value = 1 #slot pris
				}

				#selection de la bank
				random_in_list = {
					variable = privat_bank_variable_list_ordered
     			   	save_scope_as = buyer_privat_bank
				}

				# Pour les liste du vendeur
				add_to_variable_list = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_country_20
					target = scope:buyer_privat_bank #prev
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_20
					target = scope:seller #prev
				}

				######
				#quantité
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_20
					value = bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank
				}

				#valeur de la quantité
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_20
					value = 1
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_20
					multiply = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_20 #bond quantité
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_20
					multiply = scope:seller.bond_price_in_gold
				}

				#interet total
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_20
					value = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_20
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_20
					multiply = scope:seller.interest_value_10Y
				}
				add_investment_pool = {
					subtract = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_20
				}
			}

			#################
			#notification
			every_country = {
				limit  = {
					is_player = yes
				}
				save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_selle_bond_maturity_20_message
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:buyer = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_buy_bond_maturity_20_message
				}
			}

			#central_bank_interest_refund_per_year_in_gold = yes

			#################
			#ajout à la liste des acheteur
			add_to_variable_list = {
				name =  central_bank_debt_privat_bank_buyer_list_20 #plusieur bank peuvent etre stack dans cette liste
				target = scope:buyer_privat_bank
			}
		}
    }
}

				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_19 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_19 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_20 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_20 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_21 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_21 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_22 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_22 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_23 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_23 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_24 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_24 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_25 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_25 }
			}
        }
		scope:seller = {
			scope:buyer = {
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_slot_22
					value = 1 #slot pris
				}

				#selection de la bank
				random_in_list = {
					variable = privat_bank_variable_list_ordered
     			   	save_scope_as = buyer_privat_bank
				}

				# Pour les liste du vendeur
				add_to_variable_list = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_country_22
					target = scope:buyer_privat_bank #prev
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_22
					target = scope:seller #prev
				}

				######
				#quantité
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_22
					value = bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank
				}

				#valeur de la quantité
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_22
					value = 1
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_22
					multiply = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_22 #bond quantité
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_22
					multiply = scope:seller.bond_price_in_gold
				}

				#interet total
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_22
					value = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_22
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_22
					multiply = scope:seller.interest_value_10Y
				}
				add_investment_pool = {
					subtract = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_22
				}
			}

			#################
			#notification
			every_country = {
				limit  = {
					is_player = yes
				}
				save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_selle_bond_maturity_22_message
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:buyer = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_buy_bond_maturity_22_message
				}
			}

			#central_bank_interest_refund_per_year_in_gold = yes

			#################
			#ajout à la liste des acheteur
			add_to_variable_list = {
				name =  central_bank_debt_privat_bank_buyer_list_22 #plusieur bank peuvent etre stack dans cette liste
				target = scope:buyer_privat_bank
			}
		}
    }
}

				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_21 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_21 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_22 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_22 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_23 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_23 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_24 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_24 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_25 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_25 }
			}
        }
		scope:seller = {
			scope:buyer = {
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_slot_24
					value = 1 #slot pris
				}

				#selection de la bank
				random_in_list = {
					variable = privat_bank_variable_list_ordered
     			   	save_scope_as = buyer_privat_bank
				}

				# Pour les liste du vendeur
				add_to_variable_list = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_country_24
					target = scope:buyer_privat_bank #prev
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_24
					target = scope:seller #prev
				}

				######
				#quantité
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_24
					value = bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank
				}

				#valeur de la quantité
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_24
					value = 1
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_24
					multiply = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_24 #bond quantité
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_24
					multiply = scope:seller.bond_price_in_gold
				}

				#interet total
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_24
					value = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_24
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_24
					multiply = scope:seller.interest_value_10Y
				}
				add_investment_pool = {
					subtract = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_24
				}
			}

			#################
			#notification
			every_country = {
				limit  = {
					is_player = yes
				}
				save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_selle_bond_maturity_24_message
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:buyer = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_buy_bond_maturity_24_message
				}
			}

			#central_bank_interest_refund_per_year_in_gold = yes

			#################
			#ajout à la liste des acheteur
			add_to_variable_list = {
				name =  central_bank_debt_privat_bank_buyer_list_24 #plusieur bank peuvent etre stack dans cette liste
				target = scope:buyer_privat_bank
			}
		}
    }
}

				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_15 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_15 }

				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_22 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_22 }

		scope:seller = {

					name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_slot_25

				}

				random_in_list = {
					variable = privat_bank_variable_list_ordered
     			   	save_scope_as = buyer_privat_bank

					name = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_25
					target = scope:seller #prev
				}

				#quantité

					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_25
					value = bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank
				}

				#valeur de la quantité
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_25
					value = 1
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_25
					multiply = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_25 #bond quantité
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_25
					multiply = scope:seller.bond_price_in_gold
				}

				#interet total
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_25
					value = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_25
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_25
					multiply = scope:seller.interest_value_10Y
				}
				add_investment_pool = {
					subtract = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_25
				}
			}

			#################
			#notification
			every_country = {
				limit  = {
					is_player = yes
				}
				save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_selle_bond_maturity_25_message
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:buyer = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_buy_bond_maturity_25_message
				}
			}

			#central_bank_interest_refund_per_year_in_gold = yes

			#################

				name =  central_bank_debt_privat_bank_buyer_list_25 #plusieur bank peuvent etre stack dans cette liste
				target = scope:buyer_privat_bank
			}
		}

#_1 -> à modifier car doit aussi comprendre un netoyage des varriable si uz bank disparée
ai_privat_bank_bond_1_renforcement = {
    root = {
        save_scope_as = buyer
    }

	random_in_list = {
		variable = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_1
		save_scope_as = seller_1
		save_scope_as = seller # bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank and the price read scope:seller
	}

	#renforcement
    if = {
        limit = {
            exists = scope:seller_1
			scope:seller_1.remaining_debt > 0
			scope:seller_1.zz_ef_bl_room > 0
        }
		scope:seller_1 = {
			scope:buyer = {
				random_in_list = {
					variable = ai_privat_bank_buyer_country_1
     			   	save_scope_as = buyer_privat_bank
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_1
					add = bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank
				}

				#valeur de la quantité
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_1_new
					value = bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_1_new
					multiply = scope:seller.bond_price_in_gold
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_1
					add = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_1_new
				}

				if = {
					limit = {
						var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_1_new > 0
					}
					#interet total -> recalcule total
					set_variable = {
						name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_1
						value = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_1
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_1
						multiply = scope:seller.interest_value_10Y
					}
				}
				add_investment_pool = {
					subtract = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_1_new
				}
 			}

			#################
			#notification
			every_country = {
				limit  = {
					is_player = yes
				}
				save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_renforcement_selle_bond_maturity_1_message
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:buyer = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_renforcement_buy_bond_maturity_1_message
				}
			}

			central_bank_interest_refund_per_year_in_gold = yes
 		}
 	}

	#clear
    if = {
        limit = {
			not = {
				exists = scope:seller_1
			}
        }
		#Reset variable
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_slot_1
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_1
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_1
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_1
			value = 0
		}

		#Reset list
		clear_variable_list = ai_privat_bank_buyer_country_1

		clear_variable_list = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_1
		clear_variable_list =  central_bank_debt_privat_bank_buyer_list_1
    }
}

				#valeur de la quantité
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_3_new
					value = bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_3_new
					multiply = scope:seller.bond_price_in_gold
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_3
					add = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_3_new
				}

				if = {
					limit = {
						var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_3_new > 0
					}
					#interet total -> recalcule total
					set_variable = {
						name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_3
						value = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_3
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_3
						multiply = scope:seller.interest_value_10Y
					}
				}
				add_investment_pool = {
					subtract = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_3_new
				}
 			}

			#################
			#notification
			every_country = {
				limit  = {
					is_player = yes
				}
				save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_renforcement_selle_bond_maturity_3_message
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:buyer = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_renforcement_buy_bond_maturity_3_message
				}
			}

			central_bank_interest_refund_per_year_in_gold = yes
 		}
 	}

	#clear
    if = {
        limit = {
			not = {
				exists = scope:seller_3
			}
        }
		#Reset variable
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_slot_3
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_3
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_3
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_3
			value = 0
		}

		#Reset list
		clear_variable_list = ai_privat_bank_buyer_country_3

		clear_variable_list = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_3
		clear_variable_list =  central_bank_debt_privat_bank_buyer_list_3
    }
}
#_4 -> à modifier car doit aussi comprendre un netoyage des varriable si uz bank disparée
ai_privat_bank_bond_4_renforcement = {
    root = {
        save_scope_as = buyer
    }

	random_in_list = {
		variable = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_4
		save_scope_as = seller_4
		save_scope_as = seller # bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank and the price read scope:seller
	}

	#renforcement
    if = {
        limit = {
            exists = scope:seller_4
			scope:seller_4.remaining_debt > 0
			scope:seller_4.zz_ef_bl_room > 0
        }
		scope:seller_4 = {
			scope:buyer = {
				random_in_list = {
					variable = ai_privat_bank_buyer_country_4
     			   	save_scope_as = buyer_privat_bank
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_4
					add = bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank
				}

				#valeur de la quantité
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_4_new
					value = bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_4_new
					multiply = scope:seller.bond_price_in_gold
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_4
					add = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_4_new
				}

				if = {
					limit = {
						var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_4_new > 0
					}
					#interet total -> recalcule total
					set_variable = {
						name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_4
						value = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_4
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_4
						multiply = scope:seller.interest_value_10Y
					}
				}
				add_investment_pool = {
					subtract = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_4_new
				}
 			}

			#################
			#notification
			every_country = {
				limit  = {
					is_player = yes
				}
				save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_renforcement_selle_bond_maturity_4_message
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:buyer = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_renforcement_buy_bond_maturity_4_message
				}
			}

			central_bank_interest_refund_per_year_in_gold = yes
 		}
 	}

	#clear
    if = {
        limit = {
			not = {
				exists = scope:seller_4
			}
        }
		#Reset variable
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_slot_4
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_4
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_4
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_4
			value = 0
		}

		#Reset list
		clear_variable_list = ai_privat_bank_buyer_country_4

		clear_variable_list = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_4
		clear_variable_list =  central_bank_debt_privat_bank_buyer_list_4
    }
}

			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_renforcement_selle_bond_maturity_9_message
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:buyer = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_renforcement_buy_bond_maturity_9_message
				}
			}

			central_bank_interest_refund_per_year_in_gold = yes
 		}
 	}

	#clear
    if = {
        limit = {
			not = {
				exists = scope:seller_9
			}
        }
		#Reset variable
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_slot_9
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_9
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_9
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_9
			value = 0
		}

		#Reset list
		clear_variable_list = ai_privat_bank_buyer_country_9

		clear_variable_list = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_9
		clear_variable_list =  central_bank_debt_privat_bank_buyer_list_9
    }
}
#_10 -> à modifier car doit aussi comprendre un netoyage des varriable si uz bank disparée
ai_privat_bank_bond_10_renforcement = {
    root = {
        save_scope_as = buyer
    }

	random_in_list = {
		variable = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_10
		save_scope_as = seller_10
		save_scope_as = seller # bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank and the price read scope:seller
	}

	#renforcement
    if = {
        limit = {
            exists = scope:seller_10
			scope:seller_10.remaining_debt > 0
			scope:seller_10.zz_ef_bl_room > 0
        }
		scope:seller_10 = {
			scope:buyer = {
				random_in_list = {
					variable = ai_privat_bank_buyer_country_10
     			   	save_scope_as = buyer_privat_bank
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_10
					add = bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank
				}

				#valeur de la quantité
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_10_new
					value = bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_10_new
					multiply = scope:seller.bond_price_in_gold
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_10
					add = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_10_new
				}

				if = {
					limit = {
						var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_10_new > 0
					}
					#interet total -> recalcule total
					set_variable = {
						name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_10
						value = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_10
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_10
						multiply = scope:seller.interest_value_10Y
					}
				}
				add_investment_pool = {
					subtract = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_10_new
				}
 			}

			#################
			#notification
			every_country = {
				limit  = {
					is_player = yes
				}
				save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_renforcement_selle_bond_maturity_10_message
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:buyer = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_renforcement_buy_bond_maturity_10_message
				}
			}

			central_bank_interest_refund_per_year_in_gold = yes
 		}
 	}

	#clear
    if = {
        limit = {
			not = {
				exists = scope:seller_10
			}
        }
		#Reset variable
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_slot_10
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_10
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_10
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_10
			value = 0
		}

		#Reset list
		clear_variable_list = ai_privat_bank_buyer_country_10

		clear_variable_list = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_10
		clear_variable_list =  central_bank_debt_privat_bank_buyer_list_10
    }
}

			value = 0
		}

		#Reset list
		clear_variable_list = ai_privat_bank_buyer_country_15

		clear_variable_list = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_15
		clear_variable_list =  central_bank_debt_privat_bank_buyer_list_15
    }
}
#_16 -> à modifier car doit aussi comprendre un netoyage des varriable si uz bank disparée
ai_privat_bank_bond_16_renforcement = {
    root = {
        save_scope_as = buyer
    }

	random_in_list = {
		variable = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_16
		save_scope_as = seller_16
		save_scope_as = seller # bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank and the price read scope:seller
	}

	#renforcement
    if = {
        limit = {
            exists = scope:seller_16
			scope:seller_16.remaining_debt > 0
			scope:seller_16.zz_ef_bl_room > 0
        }
		scope:seller_16 = {
			scope:buyer = {
				random_in_list = {
					variable = ai_privat_bank_buyer_country_16
     			   	save_scope_as = buyer_privat_bank
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_16
					add = bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank
				}

				#valeur de la quantité
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_16_new
					value = bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_16_new
					multiply = scope:seller.bond_price_in_gold
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_16
					add = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_16_new
				}

				if = {
					limit = {
						var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_16_new > 0
					}
					#interet total -> recalcule total
					set_variable = {
						name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_16
						value = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_16
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_16
						multiply = scope:seller.interest_value_10Y
					}
				}
				add_investment_pool = {
					subtract = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_16_new
				}
 			}

			#################
			#notification
			every_country = {
				limit  = {
					is_player = yes
				}
				save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_renforcement_selle_bond_maturity_16_message
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:buyer = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_renforcement_buy_bond_maturity_16_message
				}
			}

			central_bank_interest_refund_per_year_in_gold = yes
 		}
 	}

	#clear
    if = {
        limit = {
			not = {
				exists = scope:seller_16
			}
        }
		#Reset variable
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_slot_16
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_16
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_16
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_16
			value = 0
		}

		#Reset list
		clear_variable_list = ai_privat_bank_buyer_country_16

		clear_variable_list = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_16
		clear_variable_list =  central_bank_debt_privat_bank_buyer_list_16
    }
}

				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_22
					add = bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank
				}

				#valeur de la quantité
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_22_new
					value = bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_22_new
					multiply = scope:seller.bond_price_in_gold
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_22
					add = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_22_new
				}

				if = {
					limit = {
						var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_22_new > 0
					}
					#interet total -> recalcule total
					set_variable = {
						name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_22
						value = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_22
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_22
						multiply = scope:seller.interest_value_10Y
					}
				}
				add_investment_pool = {
					subtract = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_22_new
				}
 			}

			#################
			#notification
			every_country = {
				limit  = {
					is_player = yes
				}
				save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_renforcement_selle_bond_maturity_22_message
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:buyer = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_renforcement_buy_bond_maturity_22_message
				}
			}

			central_bank_interest_refund_per_year_in_gold = yes
 		}
 	}

	#clear
    if = {
        limit = {
			not = {
				exists = scope:seller_22
			}
        }
		#Reset variable
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_slot_22
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_22
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_22
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_22
			value = 0
		}

		#Reset list
		clear_variable_list = ai_privat_bank_buyer_country_22

		clear_variable_list = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_22
		clear_variable_list =  central_bank_debt_privat_bank_buyer_list_22
    }
}
#_23 -> à modifier car doit aussi comprendre un netoyage des varriable si uz bank disparée
ai_privat_bank_bond_23_renforcement = {
    root = {
        save_scope_as = buyer
    }

	random_in_list = {
		variable = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_23
		save_scope_as = seller_23
		save_scope_as = seller # bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank and the price read scope:seller
	}

	#renforcement
    if = {
        limit = {
            exists = scope:seller_23
			scope:seller_23.remaining_debt > 0
			scope:seller_23.zz_ef_bl_room > 0
        }
		scope:seller_23 = {
			scope:buyer = {
				random_in_list = {
					variable = ai_privat_bank_buyer_country_23
     			   	save_scope_as = buyer_privat_bank
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_23
					add = bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank
				}

				#valeur de la quantité
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_23_new
					value = bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_23_new
					multiply = scope:seller.bond_price_in_gold
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_23
					add = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_23_new
				}

				if = {
					limit = {
						var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_23_new > 0
					}
					#interet total -> recalcule total
					set_variable = {
						name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_23
						value = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_23
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_23

					}

 			}

 		}
 	}

    if = {
        limit = {
			not = {
				exists = scope:seller_23
			}
        }

			name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_slot_23
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_23
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_23
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_23
			value = 0
